Session Handoff — Reading Center rebuilt as a standalone Spreeder copy: reader, library and Profile done; the remaining features still need to be tested against real Spreeder
Where it started
Joshua pasted the previous handoff, in which the Reading Center build was paused at Step 10 inside LSAT U. He then changed course twice:

* First, put it in a Teacher Workspace "Tools" room.
* Then, build it as an entirely separate web app first and integrate it into LSAT U later. "Assign" will mean granting a student access to the whole tool.

He then required it to match Spreeder exactly in colours, layout and every feature's behaviour, using reverse-engineer-anything as the source of truth. His last two messages, both interrupted, were: "do all of the features and option working and mirror it /reverse-engineer-anything does it flow perfectly and such in the same way as spreeder".
Decisions locked + what shipped

* Standalone app. It lives at `C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os-projects\reading-center`: Vite + React + TS + Tailwind, a local git repo with no remote, and data saved in the browser (`src/features/reading-center/lib/store.ts`). Commits: `d2af782` (initial), `441536b` (Spreeder rebuild), `e41d2c2` (fixes for hidden tabs, the Flow mask and chart animation).
* Tools room reverted on the LSAT U branch `feat/reading-center`. Revert commit `d5bbd69b` is in the worktree `C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os-projects\lsatu-reading-center`. Earlier commits on that branch, unpushed: Step 10 fix `8ff3641c`, steps 11–17 (`3e924766`, `2f88c0d1`, `053212ed`, `c3472072`, `dbbfa1dd`, `8ab5ace4`).
* The LSAT U copy is out of date. `src/features/reading-center/` on that branch is now far behind the standalone app. Integrating means porting the standalone code across, not merging.
* Reconstruction spec. `C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os\research\2026-10-13-spreeder-reconstruction-spec.md` replaces the visual guidance in the old build spec. It covers the palette for each theme, every screen's measurements, the text model, layout, the timing formula and the dialogs. Section 7 is build status and gaps.
* Engine ported from Spreeder's shipped code (`lib/spreeder.ts`):
   * whitespace collapsed, no paragraphs
   * sentence sections of about 2,000–2,400 characters
   * line building by measured width and Words Per Line
   * the `evenChunkDelays`, variability and punctuation (×2) rules
   * remaining time and progress formulas
* Worker timer (`lib/workerTimer.ts`). Like Spreeder, it uses a Web Worker because `setTimeout` is clamped to 1 s in hidden tabs.
* Flow differs from Spreeder on purpose. Spreeder's mask uses `timeDiff/m`, which is buggy and shows almost nothing. Ours is the intended left-to-right reveal.
* Brand assets not copied. No Spreeder logo, cover art or icon font. Ionicons (npm `ionicons@7.4.0`) and Material Icons via Google Fonts stand in.
* Goal recommendation uses Spreeder's real logic (`lib/goals.ts`): reading-test WPM, then a piecewise curve with max 2000.
* Not built (need a service): Look Up Word, Add to Wordela Word List, AI Book Summary, AI Generate, the 20,504-book catalogue, Courses content, share, Sign Out.
* Built from inference (not observed in Spreeder): the filled bookmark list, the Tag, Edit Image, Delete and Edit Profile dialogs, the reading-test text picker, charts with data, the Light-theme sidenav colours, the speedometer artwork.

Key files for next session

* `C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os\research\2026-10-13-spreeder-reconstruction-spec.md`: read first. It is the source of truth.
* `C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os\research\2026-10-12-spreeder-feature-inventory.md`: the feature list, observed live 2026-10-12.
* `C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os-projects\reading-center\CLAUDE.md`: the app's own notes.
* The app's main source files, under `C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os-projects\reading-center\src\features\reading-center\`:
   * `lib\spreeder.ts`: the reading engine.
   * `hooks\useSpreederEngine.ts`: the play loop and navigation.
   * `components\SpreederStage.tsx`: word placement and the Flow mask.
   * `components\ReaderDialogs.tsx`: Preferences, WPM, Bookmarks, the bookmark note, the action sheet, shortcuts, finish, the speed toast.
   * `components\ViewPanel.tsx` and `components\EReader.tsx`.
   * `components\AppShell.tsx`: the sidenav, top bar and theme.
   * `components\AddText.tsx`.
   * `pages\ReaderPage.tsx`, `pages\LibraryPage.tsx`, `pages\ProfilePage.tsx`, `pages\TagsPage.tsx`, `pages\CoursesPage.tsx`.
* Spreeder code dumps (scratchpad, disposable), under `C:\Users\Joshu\AppData\Local\Temp\claude\C--Users-Joshu-dev-ai-os\95fbd50f-6898-4412-a19a-7fd02386eb44\scratchpad\`:
   * `spreeder-css\main.js`, the 219 lazy chunks in `spreeder-css\chunks\`, and `all.js` (concatenated). The reader chunk is `chunks\9875.e779d5300d96c214.js`.
   * `spreeder-components\` (per-component CSS and texts) and `spreeder-code\` (dumped mode classes).
   * `jsfind.py` (context search) and `rea-spreeder-full.json` (REA run, 222 files, 2804 modules).
* Memory files touched:
   * `C:\Users\Joshu\.claude\projects\C--Users-Joshu-dev-ai-os\memory\reading-center-standalone-first.md`
   * `C:\Users\Joshu\.claude\projects\C--Users-Joshu-dev-ai-os\memory\MEMORY.md` (one line added)
* Config touched: `C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os\.claude\launch.json` gained the `reading-center-dev` config (port 8100).

Running state

* Background processes: none of mine. The REA runs, the collector server and the stray `cat` hang were all stopped or finished.
* Dev servers / ports:
   * `reading-center-dev` at http://localhost:8100, preview serverId `14406726-62cc-4630-9ffd-5cd9f10d713c`, tab `tab-1`. Stop it with `preview_stop`.
   * The 8098 LSAT U preview was stopped.
* Open worktrees / branches:
   * `C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os-projects\lsatu-reading-center` on `feat/reading-center`, unpushed. It has one uncommitted, build-mangled `supabase/functions/mcp/index.ts` (my `git checkout` restore was denied).
   * The shared checkout `C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os-projects\lsatu` was not touched. It also shows `supabase/functions/mcp/index.ts` modified.
* Claude-in-Chrome: the tab I opened on spreeder.ereflect.com is closed and its group is gone.

Verified state as of 12:38

* Standalone typecheck clean, 28/28 vitest pass, build passes: run just before commit `e41d2c2`, around 12:35.
* Verified in the browser pane:
   * library (Dark)
   * Serial playback, about 310 ms per word with punctuation doubled
   * Preferences dialog
   * Highlight
   * e-Reader, Light: 1/6 pages, search "rational" gives 3 results, highlight works
   * View panel (Light)
   * Flow: chunk timing about 900 ms for 3 words, transition running 894 ms linear
   * Scroll: one line, +36px per step, wraps after 15 lines
   * Profile: empty dashboard, charts with seeded sessions, Stats pickers, Options theme submenu, Edit Profile, Goals Set, the Recommend test flow, Settings showing only Appearance and Advanced
* Pane limits: it was hidden during testing, so the Flow sweep was never seen moving on screen. Screenshots crop to the top-left at dpr 1.25.
* Standalone app's browser storage: test sessions removed; preferences reset to serial, Words Per Line 1, Dark, goal 600; `profileName` is "Joshua" (set during the Edit Profile test).
* Joshua's real Spreeder account: theme set back to Dark and mode back to Serial, checked in its prefs dialog around 09:00. Its reading position moved forward a few words while testing Scroll. No bookmark, goal, text or deletion changes.
* NOT re-verified in this session:
   * Add Text (Paste and Import)
   * library ⋮ actions (View/Location, Edit Image, Edit Text, Tag, Delete, Hide from Recent)
   * Tags filtering
   * bookmark create, edit and delete
   * WPM dialog
   * keyboard shortcuts popover and keys
   * word action sheet
   * finish alert
   * Speed Variability timing
   * High Contrast and System themes in the standalone app
   * fullscreen
* LSAT U migration `20261013120000_reading_center_v1.sql`: never applied, not needed until integration.

Verification — how to confirm things still work

* `cd C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os-projects/reading-center && npm run typecheck`: expect a clean pass.
* `cd C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os-projects/reading-center && npx vitest run`: expect 28 passing.
* `git -C C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os-projects/reading-center log --oneline -3`: expect `e41d2c2` on top.
* Open http://localhost:8100/read/living-in-a-rational-society, press Play, and time the word changes: expect about 300 ms per word at 200 WPM, doubled on punctuation.

Deferred + open questions

* OPEN: Joshua's last request (interrupted twice): make every feature and option work exactly like Spreeder, verified with reverse-engineer-anything, including whether it "flows" the same. That needs a side-by-side behavioural check against live Spreeder (his logged-in Chrome) of every untested item listed above.
* OPEN: Look Up Word / Add to Wordela Word List, AI Book Summary / AI Generate, and Courses all need an outside service or Spreeder data. Ask Joshua whether to build substitutes (for example a dictionary API) or leave them out.
* OPEN: Should Flow copy Spreeder's actual (buggy) output instead of the intended reveal? It currently does the intended reveal, as described above.
* OPEN: confirm "Joshua" as the profile name, or change it.
* OPEN: the mangled `supabase/functions/mcp/index.ts` in `lsatu-reading-center` needs `git checkout -- supabase/functions/mcp/index.ts`. Joshua runs it, or approves it.
* Deferred: porting the standalone app into LSAT U, the access flag, and applying the migration, all after the standalone app is signed off.
* Deferred: adding Ghidra and JADX to `C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os\references\ai-workspace\windows-cli-toolchain.md`, from the previous handoff, still unanswered.
* Deferred: webfonts for OpenDyslexic, Antikor, CQ Mono and the other non-system fonts.
* Note: the REA MCP server failed to connect this session. The CLI `npx -y rea-agents@6.4.0` works.

Pick up here
Raise the OPEN items. Then go through each untested feature side by side in real Spreeder (Claude in Chrome, spreeder.ereflect.com) and the standalone app at http://localhost:8100. Fix every behavioural difference and record the results in section 7 of the reconstruction spec.

10	/** Spreeder themes the whole app from the reading theme. */
11	export function useAppTheme(): void {
12	  const theme: ReaderTheme = usePreferences().data?.theme ?? "system";
13	  useEffect(() => {
14	    const mq = window.matchMedia("(prefers-color-scheme: dark)");
15	    const apply = () => {
16	      const resolved = theme === "system" ? (mq.matches ? "dark" : "light") : theme;
17	      document.documentElement.dataset.rcTheme = resolved;
18	    };
19	    apply();
20	    mq.addEventListener("change", apply);
21	    return () => mq.removeEventListener("change", apply);
22	  }, [theme]);
23	}

271	/** Ionic action sheet Spreeder opens on a word click. Look Up Word / Wordela lists are not built yet. */
272	export function WordActionSheet({ word, onClose, onBookmark }: { word: Word | null; onClose: () => void; onBookmark: (w: Word) => void }): React.ReactElement | null {
273	  if (!word) return null;
274	  const header = word.text.replace(/[^\w\s'-]/g, "").replace(/'s$/i, "").trim();
275	  return createPortal(
276	    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-end justify-center" style={{ background: "rgba(0,0,0,.32)" }} onMouseDown={(e) => e.target === e.currentTarget && onClose()}>
277	      <div role="dialog" aria-label={header} className="w-full max-w-[500px] pb-2" style={{ background: "var(--rc-menu)", color: "var(--rc-text)", boxShadow: "0 -2px 8px rgba(0,0,0,.2)" }}>
278	        <div className="flex h-14 items-center px-4 text-base" style={{ color: "var(--rc-text-2)" }}>{header}</div>
279	        <button type="button" className="rc-menu-item h-[52px] text-base" onClick={() => onBookmark(word)}>
280	          <Icon name="bookmark" size={24} style={{ color: "var(--rc-text-2)" }} /> Bookmark/Note
281	        </button>
282	        <button type="button" className="rc-menu-item h-[52px] text-base" onClick={onClose}>
283	          <MIcon name="cancel" style={{ color: "var(--rc-text-2)" }} /> Cancel
284	        </button>
285	      </div>
286	    </div>,
287	    document.body,
288	  );
289	}
290	
291	// ---------------------------------------------------------------- shortcuts
292	
293	function Key({ children, wide = false, tall = false }: { children: React.ReactNode; wide?: boolean; tall?: boolean }): React.ReactElement {
294	  return (
295	    <kbd
296	      className="inline-flex items-center justify-center rounded text-[13px] font-medium"
297	      style={{
298	        width: wide ? 216 : 32,
299	        height: tall ? 48 : 30,
300	        background: "var(--rc-keycap)",
301	        color: "var(--rc-strong)",
302	        boxShadow: "0 2px 0 rgba(0,0,0,.25)",
303	        fontFamily: "Roboto, Poppins, sans-serif",
304	      }}
305	    >
306	      {children}
307	    </kbd>
308	  );
309	}
310	
311	export function ShortcutsPopover({ open, anchorX, onClose }: { open: boolean; anchorX: number; onClose: () => void }): React.ReactElement | null {
312	  useEffect(() => {
313	    if (!open) return;
314	    const close = (e: MouseEvent) => !(e.target as HTMLElement).closest("[data-rc-shortcuts]") && onClose();
315	    window.addEventListener("mousedown", close);
316	    return () => window.removeEventListener("mousedown", close);
317	  }, [open, onClose]);
318	  if (!open) return null;
319	  const lbl = "text-xs";

"""jsfind.py <file> <literal> [before] [after] [max]: print context around each literal hit, ';' -> newline."""
import sys

path, needle = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2]
before = int(sys.argv[3]) if len(sys.argv) > 3 else 300
after = int(sys.argv[4]) if len(sys.argv) > 4 else 300
limit = int(sys.argv[5]) if len(sys.argv) > 5 else 5
s = open(path, encoding="utf-8").read()
i, n = 0, 0
while n < limit:
    i = s.find(needle, i)
    if i < 0:
        break
    print(f"===== @{i}")
    print(s[max(0, i - before): i + after].replace(";", ";\n"))
    i += len(needle)
    n += 1
[/external_agent_tool_result]

import React from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { usePassages } from "../hooks/useReadingCenterData";
import { rcTagPath } from "../lib/routes";
import { Icon } from "../components/Icon";
import { TopBar } from "../components/AppShell";

/** Tags: every tag used on a text; opening one filters the library (Spreeder library/tags/:name). */
export default function TagsPage(): React.ReactElement {
  const passages = usePassages().data ?? [];
  const counts = new Map<string, number>();
  for (const p of passages) for (const t of p.tags ?? []) counts.set(t, (counts.get(t) ?? 0) + 1);
  const tags = [...counts.entries()].sort((a, b) => a[0].localeCompare(b[0]));
  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen">
      <TopBar />
      <div className="px-5">
        <h2 className="mb-4 text-lg font-bold" style={{ color: "var(--rc-strong)" }}>Tags</h2>
        {tags.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="mt-16 text-center text-base" style={{ color: "var(--rc-text-2)" }}>No tags yet. Use Tag on a text's ⋮ menu to add one.</p>
        ) : (
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
            {tags.map(([t, n]) => (
              <Link key={t} to={rcTagPath(t)} className="flex h-12 items-center gap-2 rounded-lg px-4 text-sm font-medium" style={{ background: "var(--rc-card)", color: "var(--rc-strong)" }}>
                <Icon name="pricetag" size={16} /> {t} <span style={{ color: "var(--rc-text-2)" }}>({n})</span>
              </Link>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
import { SEED_PASSAGE } from "../data/seed";
import { DEFAULT_PREFERENCES } from "./constants";
import { countWords } from "./tokenize";
import type { Bookmark, Passage, Preferences, Progress, ReadingSession } from "./types";

/**
 * Browser storage for the standalone app: one JSON document in localStorage.
 * It plays the part of the five reading_center_* tables. On integration into
 * LSAT U, hooks/useReadingCenterData.ts goes back to Supabase and this file
 * is deleted; nothing else reads it except the passage-manager delete check.
 */
interface Db {
  version: 1;
  passages: Passage[];
  preferences: Preferences;
  progress: Progress[];
  bookmarks: Bookmark[];
  sessions: ReadingSession[];
  /** Shown as "<name>'s preferences" and "<name>'s Spreeder"-style headings. */
  profileName?: string;
}

const KEY = "reading-center:v1";

function seedDb(): Db {
  const now = new Date().toISOString();
  return {
    version: 1,
    passages: [
      {
        id: crypto.randomUUID(),
        slug: SEED_PASSAGE.slug,
        title: SEED_PASSAGE.title,
        author: null,
        body: SEED_PASSAGE.body,
        cover_url: null,
        word_count: countWords(SEED_PASSAGE.body),
        sort_order: 1,
        is_published: true,
        created_at: now,
        updated_at: now,
      },
    ],
    preferences: DEFAULT_PREFERENCES,
    progress: [],
    bookmarks: [],
    sessions: [],
  };
}

let memory: Db | null = null;

export function readDb(): Db {
  if (memory) return memory;
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(KEY);
    if (raw) {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as Db;
      memory = { ...seedDb(), ...parsed, preferences: { ...DEFAULT_PREFERENCES, ...parsed.preferences } };
      return memory;
    }
  } catch {
    // Blocked or corrupt storage: fall through to a fresh in-memory copy.
  }
  memory = seedDb();
  writeDb(memory);
  return memory;
}

export function writeDb(next: Db): void {
  memory = next;
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(KEY, JSON.stringify(next));
  } catch {
    // Storage full or blocked: keep working from memory for this tab.
  }
}

export function updateDb(fn: (db: Db) => Db): Db {
  const next = fn(readDb());

164	// ---------------------------------------------------------------- bookmarks
165	
166	export function BookmarksDialog({
167	  open,
168	  onClose,
169	  bookmarks,
170	  words,
171	  onOpenBookmark,
172	}: {
173	  open: boolean;
174	  onClose: () => void;
175	  bookmarks: Bookmark[];
176	  words: Word[];
177	  onOpenBookmark: (b: Bookmark) => void;
178	}): React.ReactElement {
179	  const sorted = [...bookmarks].sort((a, b) => a.word_index - b.word_index);
180	  return (
181	    <Modal open={open} onClose={onClose} width={850} height={480} closeOnEscape labelledBy="rc-bm-title">
182	      <DialogHeader id="rc-bm-title" title="Bookmarks" onClose={onClose} />
183	      {sorted.length === 0 ? (
184	        <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col items-center justify-center pb-8 text-center">
185	          <div className="flex h-[150px] w-[150px] items-center justify-center rounded-full" style={{ background: "#757575" }}>
186	            <Icon name="list" size={84} style={{ color: "#fff" }} />
187	          </div>
188	          <div className="mt-4 text-[28px]" style={{ color: "#616161" }}>No Bookmarks Yet</div>
189	          <p className="mt-3 max-w-[760px] text-base font-medium" style={{ color: "#757575" }}>
190	            To create a bookmark, simply pause Spreeder while you're reading and long press or click on any word.
191	          </p>
192	        </div>
193	      ) : (
194	        <ul className="mt-4 flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-5">
195	          {sorted.map((b) => (
196	            <li key={b.id} className="border-b" style={{ borderColor: "#cccccc" }}>
197	              <button type="button" onClick={() => onOpenBookmark(b)} className="flex w-full items-center gap-4 py-3 text-left text-sm hover:bg-[var(--rc-menu-hover)]">
198	                <span className="w-[30%] truncate font-medium">{words[b.word_index]?.text.replace(/[^\w\s'-]/g, "")}</span>
199	                <span className="w-[50%] truncate" style={{ color: "var(--rc-text-2)" }}>{b.note || ""}</span>
200	                <span className="w-[20%] text-right" style={{ color: "var(--rc-text-2)" }}>{new Date(b.created_at).toLocaleDateString()}</span>
201	              </button>
202	            </li>
203	          ))}
204	        </ul>
205	      )}
206	    </Modal>
207	  );
208	}

import React, { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { createPortal } from "react-dom";
import type { Bookmark, Preferences, ReaderTheme, ReadingMode } from "../lib/types";
import type { Word } from "../lib/spreeder";
import { DEFAULT_PREFERENCES, FONTS, FONT_SIZES, READING_MODES, THEMES } from "../lib/constants";
import { clampWpm } from "../lib/spreeder";
import { DialogHeader, Dropdown, HelpTip, Modal, PillTabs, TickSlider, Toggle } from "./spreeder-ui";
import { Icon, MIcon } from "./Icon";

const fontStackOf = (label: string): string => FONTS.find((f) => f.label === label)?.stack ?? FONTS[0].stack;

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- preferences

type PrefTab = "spreed" | "appearance" | "advanced";

}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- finish + toast

export function FinishAlert({ title, open, onOkay }: { title: string; open: boolean; onOkay: () => void }): React.ReactElement | null {
  if (!open) return null;
  return createPortal(
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center" style={{ background: "rgba(0,0,0,.32)" }}>
      <div role="alertdialog" aria-labelledby="rc-finish-h" className="w-[280px] rounded p-5 pb-2" style={{ background: "var(--rc-dialog)", color: "var(--rc-text)", boxShadow: "0 11px 15px -7px rgba(0,0,0,.2), 0 24px 38px 3px rgba(0,0,0,.14)" }}>
        <h2 id="rc-finish-h" className="text-base font-medium" style={{ color: "var(--rc-text-2)" }}>Congratulations!</h2>
        <p className="mt-3 text-sm" style={{ color: "var(--rc-text-2)" }}>
          You have successfully completed {title}. It will now be moved to your finished section on the Home Screen.
        </p>
        <div className="mt-4 flex justify-end">
          <button type="button" className="h-9 rounded px-3 text-sm font-medium uppercase" style={{ color: "#3880ff" }} onClick={onOkay}>Okay</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>,
    document.body,
  );
}

/** "N WPM" pill: fades in .5s, holds 2s, fades out 2s (Spreeder updateNotificationText). */
export function SpeedToast({ text, nonce }: { text: string; nonce: number }): React.ReactElement {
  const [visible, setVisible] = useState(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!nonce) return;
    setVisible(true);
    const t = window.setTimeout(() => setVisible(false), 2000);
    return () => window.clearTimeout(t);
  }, [nonce]);
  return (
    <div
      aria-live="polite"
      className="pointer-events-none absolute left-1/2 top-[10px] z-[4] -translate-x-1/2 whitespace-nowrap rounded px-2 py-2 text-sm text-white"
      style={{ background: "#323232", opacity: visible ? 1 : 0, transition: visible ? "opacity .5s" : "opacity 2s" }}
    >
      {text}
    </div>
  );
}

}

14:export function progressCountForPassage(passageId: string): number {
import { useQuery, useMutation, useQueryClient, type UseQueryResult, type UseMutationResult } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { readDb, updateDb } from "../lib/store";
import { countWords, slugify } from "../lib/tokenize";
import type { Passage, Preferences, Progress, Bookmark, ReadingSession } from "../lib/types";

/**
 * Same hook names and shapes as the LSAT U (Supabase) version, backed by
 * browser storage. Integration swaps this one file back.
 */

const byOrder = (a: Passage, b: Passage): number => a.sort_order - b.sort_order || a.title.localeCompare(b.title);

export function usePassages(): UseQueryResult<Passage[], Error> {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ["reading-center", "passages"],
    queryFn: async (): Promise<Passage[]> => [...readDb().passages].sort(byOrder),
  });
}

export function usePassageBySlug(slug: string | undefined): UseQueryResult<Passage | null, Error> {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ["reading-center", "passage", slug],
    queryFn: async (): Promise<Passage | null> => readDb().passages.find((p) => p.slug === slug) ?? null,
    enabled: !!slug,
  });
}

export function usePreferences(): UseQueryResult<Preferences, Error> {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ["reading-center", "prefs"],
      updateDb((db) => ({ ...db, sessions: [...db.sessions, { id: crypto.randomUUID(), ...session }] }));
    },
    onSuccess: () => queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["reading-center", "sessions"] }),
  });
}

export function useUpsertPassage(): UseMutationResult<
  Passage,
  Error,
  Partial<Passage> & { title: string; body: string }
> {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (input: Partial<Passage> & { title: string; body: string }): Promise<Passage> => {
      const now = new Date().toISOString();
      let saved: Passage | null = null;
      updateDb((db) => {
        const existing = input.id ? db.passages.find((p) => p.id === input.id) : undefined;
        const slug = existing?.slug ?? input.slug ?? slugify(input.title);
        if (!existing && db.passages.some((p) => p.slug === slug)) {
          throw new Error(`A passage with the address "${slug}" already exists. Change the title.`);
        }
        saved = {
          id: existing?.id ?? crypto.randomUUID(),
          author: null,
          cover_url: null,
          is_published: true,
          sort_order: db.passages.reduce((m, p) => Math.max(m, p.sort_order), 0) + 1,
          created_at: now,
          ...existing,
          ...input,
          slug,
          word_count: countWords(input.body),
          updated_at: now,
        } as Passage;
        return { ...db, passages: [...db.passages.filter((p) => p.id !== saved!.id), saved] };
      });
      return saved!;
    },
    onSuccess: () => queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["reading-center"] }),
  });
}

export function useDeletePassage(): UseMutationResult<void, Error, string> {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (id: string): Promise<void> => {
      // Mirrors the on-delete-cascade in the LSAT U migration.
      updateDb((db) => ({
        ...db,
        passages: db.passages.filter((p) => p.id !== id),
        progress: db.progress.filter((p) => p.passage_id !== id),
        bookmarks: db.bookmarks.filter((b) => b.passage_id !== id),
        sessions: db.sessions.filter((s) => s.passage_id !== id),
      }));
    },
    onSuccess: () => queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["reading-center"] }),
  });
}

/**
 * Standalone app paths, mirroring Spreeder (/library, /spreed, /profile, ...).
 * On integration into LSAT U these become the LSAT U paths; every page links
 * through these constants, so this is the only file that changes.
 */
export const RC_LIBRARY_PATH = "/";
export const RC_TAGS_PATH = "/tags";
export const RC_COURSES_PATH = "/courses";
export const RC_PROFILE_PATH = "/profile";
export const RC_STATS_PATH = "/profile";
export const RC_MANAGE_PATH = "/manage";

export function rcReaderPath(slug: string): string {
  return `/read/${slug}`;
}

export function rcTagPath(tag: string): string {
  return `/tags/${encodeURIComponent(tag)}`;
}
import { BrowserRouter, Route, Routes } from "react-router-dom";
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { Toaster } from "sonner";
import { AppShell, useAppTheme } from "@/features/reading-center/components/AppShell";
import LibraryPage from "@/features/reading-center/pages/LibraryPage";
import ReaderPage from "@/features/reading-center/pages/ReaderPage";
import ProfilePage from "@/features/reading-center/pages/ProfilePage";
import TagsPage from "@/features/reading-center/pages/TagsPage";
import CoursesPage from "@/features/reading-center/pages/CoursesPage";

const queryClient = new QueryClient({ defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: false, refetchOnWindowFocus: false } } });

function ThemedRoutes() {
  useAppTheme();
  return (
    <Routes>
      <Route element={<AppShell />}>
        <Route path="/" element={<LibraryPage />} />
        <Route path="/tags" element={<TagsPage />} />
        <Route path="/tags/:tag" element={<LibraryPage />} />
        <Route path="/courses" element={<CoursesPage />} />
        <Route path="/profile" element={<ProfilePage />} />
      </Route>
      <Route path="/read/:slug" element={<ReaderPage />} />
      <Route path="*" element={<LibraryPage />} />
    </Routes>
  );
}

export default function App() {
  return (
    <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>
      <BrowserRouter>
        <ThemedRoutes />
      </BrowserRouter>
      <Toaster position="bottom-center" />
    </QueryClientProvider>
  );
}
export type ReadingMode = "serial" | "flow" | "highlight" | "scroll" | "ereader";
export type ReaderTheme = "light" | "dark" | "high_contrast" | "system";

export interface Passage {
  id: string;
  slug: string;
  title: string;
  author: string | null;
  body: string;
  cover_url: string | null;
  word_count: number;
  sort_order: number;
  is_published: boolean;
  /** Spreeder tags (standalone only for now). */
  tags?: string[];
  created_at: string;
  updated_at: string;
}

export interface Preferences {
  wpm: number;
  reading_mode: ReadingMode;
  number_of_lines: number;
  words_per_line: number;
  center_text: boolean;
  theme: ReaderTheme;
  font_family: string;
  font_size: number;
  line_spacing: number;
  punctuation_pauses: boolean;
  speed_variability: boolean;
  goal_wpm: number;
}

export interface Progress {
  passage_id: string;
  word_index: number;
  completed_at: string | null;
  last_opened_at: string;
  hidden_from_recent: boolean;
  const next = fn(readDb());
  writeDb(next);
  return next;
}

export function profileName(): string {
  return readDb().profileName || "Reader";
}

export function setProfileName(name: string): void {
  updateDb((db) => ({ ...db, profileName: name.trim() || "Reader" }));
}

export function progressCountForPassage(passageId: string): number {
  return readDb().progress.filter((p) => p.passage_id === passageId && p.word_index > 0).length;
}

// ---- tags (Spreeder UserTags; standalone only for now)

/** Every user tag in creation order, plus any name a passage carries that is not in the list. */
export function useTags(): UseQueryResult<UserTag[], Error> {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ["reading-center", "tags"],
    queryFn: async (): Promise<UserTag[]> => {
      const db = readDb();
      const list = [...(db.tags ?? [])];
      for (const p of db.passages) for (const t of p.tags ?? []) if (!list.some((x) => x.name === t)) list.push({ name: t, color: 0 });
      return list;
    },
  });
}

/** Create a tag, or rename / recolour one (oldName set). Renames carry through to passages. */
export function useSaveTag(): UseMutationResult<UserTag, Error, { oldName?: string; name: string; color?: number }> {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async ({ oldName, name, color }): Promise<UserTag> => {
      const clean = name.trim();
      if (!clean) throw new Error("Enter a tag name.");
      let saved: UserTag = { name: clean, color: color ?? 0 };
      updateDb((db) => {
        const tags = [...(db.tags ?? [])];
        for (const p of db.passages) for (const t of p.tags ?? []) if (!tags.some((x) => x.name === t)) tags.push({ name: t, color: 0 });
        const i = oldName != null ? tags.findIndex((t) => t.name === oldName) : -1;
        if (tags.some((t, j) => t.name === clean && j !== i)) throw new Error(`A tag named "${clean}" already exists.`);
        if (i >= 0) {
          saved = { name: clean, color: color ?? tags[i].color };
          tags[i] = saved;
        } else tags.push(saved);
        const passages = oldName != null && oldName !== clean
          ? db.passages.map((p) => (p.tags?.includes(oldName) ? { ...p, tags: p.tags.map((t) => (t === oldName ? clean : t)) } : p))
          : db.passages;
        return { ...db, tags, passages };
      });
      return saved;
    },
    onSuccess: () => queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["reading-center"] }),
  });
}

export function useDeleteTag(): UseMutationResult<void, Error, string> {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (name: string): Promise<void> => {
      updateDb((db) => ({
        ...db,
        tags: (db.tags ?? []).filter((t) => t.name !== name),
        passages: db.passages.map((p) => (p.tags?.includes(name) ? { ...p, tags: p.tags.filter((t) => t !== name) } : p)),
      }));
    },
    onSuccess: () => queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["reading-center"] }),
  });
}

12	export function Modal({
13	  open,
14	  onClose,
15	  width,
16	  height,
17	  children,
18	  closeOnBackdrop = false,
19	  closeOnEscape = false,
20	  fullScreen = false,
21	  labelledBy,
22	}: {
23	  open: boolean;
24	  onClose: () => void;
25	  width?: number | string;
26	  height?: number | string;
27	  children: React.ReactNode;
28	  closeOnBackdrop?: boolean;
29	  closeOnEscape?: boolean;
30	  fullScreen?: boolean;
31	  labelledBy?: string;
32	}): React.ReactElement | null {
33	  useEffect(() => {
34	    if (!open || !closeOnEscape) return;
35	    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => e.key === "Escape" && onClose();
36	    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
37	    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);

43	      style={{ background: fullScreen ? "transparent" : "var(--rc-backdrop)" }}
44	      onMouseDown={(e) => closeOnBackdrop && e.target === e.currentTarget && onClose()}

  const [navOpen, setNavOpen] = useState(true);
  const [addOpen, setAddOpen] = useState(false);
  const [screen, setScreen] = useState<"paste" | "import" | null>(null);
  const [creatingTag, setCreatingTag] = useState(false);
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const onTags = useLocation().pathname.startsWith(RC_TAGS_PATH);
  // Spreeder collapses the sidenav to a 90px icon rail rather than hiding it.
  const rail = !navOpen;

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen" style={{ background: "var(--rc-page)", color: "var(--rc-text)" }}>
      <aside
        className="sticky top-0 h-screen shrink-0 overflow-hidden transition-[width] duration-200"
        style={{ width: rail ? 90 : 230, background: "var(--rc-sidenav)", borderRight: "0.8px solid rgba(128,128,128,.15)" }}
      >
        <div className="flex h-[60px] items-center">
          <button type="button" aria-label={rail ? "Show menu" : "Hide menu"} onClick={() => setNavOpen((o) => !o)} className="ml-[15px] flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-full hover:bg-[rgba(158,158,158,.2)]" style={{ color: "var(--rc-nav-text)" }}>
            <Icon name="menu" size={24} />
          </button>
          {!rail && <span className="ml-2.5 whitespace-nowrap text-[19px] font-semibold leading-none tracking-tight" style={{ color: "var(--rc-nav-text)" }}>Reading Center</span>}
        </div>
        {onTags ? (
          <TagsNav rail={rail} onBack={() => navigate(RC_LIBRARY_PATH)} onCreate={() => setCreatingTag(true)} />
        ) : (
          <>
            <div className="relative px-5 pt-[10px]">
              <button
                type="button"
                aria-label="Add Text"
                onClick={() => setAddOpen((o) => !o)}
                className="flex h-[55px] items-center rounded-xl text-sm font-medium"
                style={{ width: rail ? 49 : 189, background: "var(--rc-addtext-bg)", color: "var(--rc-addtext-fg)" }}
              >
                <Icon name="addCircle" size={22} className={rail ? "mx-auto" : "ml-[16px]"} />
                {!rail && <span className="ml-[30px] leading-8">Add Text</span>}
              </button>
              {addOpen && (
                <AddTextMenu
                  onClose={() => setAddOpen(false)}
                  onPick={(s) => {
                    setAddOpen(false);
                    setScreen(s);
                  }}
                />
              )}
            </div>
            <nav aria-label="Main" className="mt-[22px] space-y-1 px-5">
              {NAV.map((n) => (
                <NavLink
                  key={n.to}
                  to={n.to}
                  end={n.end}
                  title={rail ? n.label : undefined}
                  aria-label={n.label}
                  className="relative flex h-[52px] items-center rounded-lg text-sm font-medium transition-colors hover:bg-[var(--rc-nav-hover)]"
                  style={({ isActive }) => ({
                    width: rail ? 49 : 189,
                    background: isActive ? "var(--rc-nav-active-bg)" : undefined,
                    color: isActive ? "var(--rc-nav-active-fg)" : "var(--rc-nav-text)",
                  })}
                >
                  {({ isActive }) => (
                    <>
                      {isActive && <span className="absolute inset-y-0 left-0 w-1 rounded-l-lg" style={{ background: "var(--rc-nav-active-fg)" }} />}
                      <Icon name={n.icon} size={18} className={rail ? "mx-auto" : "ml-[16px]"} />
                      {!rail && <span className="ml-[17px]" style={{ color: isActive ? "var(--rc-nav-text)" : undefined }}>{n.label}</span>}
                    </>
                  )}
                </NavLink>
              ))}
            </nav>
          </>
        )}
      </aside>
      <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
        <Outlet />
      </div>
      {screen && <AddTextScreen mode={screen} onClose={() => setScreen(null)} onSaved={(slug) => { setScreen(null); navigate(RC_LIBRARY_PATH); void slug; }} />}
      {creatingTag && <TagEditDialog onClose={() => setCreatingTag(false)} onSaved={(t) => navigate(rcTagPath(t.name))} />}
    </div>
  );
}

/** Spreeder tags-nav-menu: Back (green pill), Create New Tag (blue), then one row per tag with a menu. */
function TagsNav({ rail, onBack, onCreate }: { rail: boolean; onBack: () => void; onCreate: () => void }): React.ReactElement {
  const tags = useTags().data ?? [];
  const saveTag = useSaveTag();
  const deleteTag = useDeleteTag();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { tag: current } = useParams<{ tag?: string }>();
  const [menu, setMenu] = useState<{ tag: UserTag; x: number; y: number; colours: boolean } | null>(null);
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<UserTag | null>(null);
  const [deleting, setDeleting] = useState<UserTag | null>(null);
  const pill = "flex h-[55px] items-center rounded-xl text-sm font-medium";
  return (
    <div className="px-5 pt-[9px]">
      <button type="button" aria-label="Back" onClick={onBack} className={pill} style={{ width: rail ? 49 : 189, background: "var(--rc-nav-active-bg)", color: "var(--rc-nav-text)" }}>
        <MIcon name="arrow_circle_left" size={22} className={rail ? "mx-auto" : "ml-[14px]"} style={{ color: "var(--rc-nav-active-fg)" }} />
        {!rail && <span className="ml-[16px]">Back</span>}
      </button>
      <button type="button" aria-label="Create New Tag" onClick={onCreate} className={`${pill} mt-[11px]`} style={{ width: rail ? 49 : 189, background: "var(--rc-addtext-bg)", color: "var(--rc-addtext-fg)" }}>
        <Icon name="addCircle" size={22} className={rail ? "mx-auto" : "ml-[14px]"} />
        {!rail && <span className="ml-[8px]">Create New Tag</span>}
      </button>
      <ul className="mt-2">
        {tags.map((t) => {
          const active = t.name === current;
          return (
            <li key={t.name} className="group relative flex h-14 items-center" title={rail ? t.name : undefined}>
              <span
                className="absolute inset-x-0 top-1/2 h-12 -translate-y-1/2 overflow-hidden rounded-lg group-hover:visible"
                style={{ visibility: active ? "visible" : undefined, background: active ? "var(--rc-nav-active-bg)" : "var(--rc-nav-hover)" }}
              >
                {active && <span className="block h-full w-1" style={{ background: "#3aab1f" }} />}
              </span>
              <button type="button" onClick={() => navigate(rcTagPath(t.name))} className="relative z-[1] flex h-full flex-1 items-center pl-4 text-left text-sm font-medium" style={{ color: active ? "#3aab1f" : "var(--rc-nav-text)" }}>
                <TagGlyph color={t.color} />
                {!rail && <span className="ml-[5px] w-[110px] truncate">{t.name}</span>}
              </button>
              {!rail && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  aria-label={`Options for ${t.name}`}
                  className="relative z-[1] mr-1 flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-full"
                  style={{ color: "var(--rc-nav-text)" }}
                  onClick={(e) => {
                    const r = e.currentTarget.getBoundingClientRect();
                    setMenu({ tag: t, x: r.left, y: r.bottom, colours: false });
                  }}
                >
                  <MIcon name="more_vert" size={20} />
                </button>
              )}
            </li>
          );
        })}
      </ul>
      {menu && (
        <PopMenu x={menu.x} y={menu.y} width={menu.colours ? 260 : 200} onClose={() => setMenu(null)}>
          {menu.colours ? (
            <TagColorPicker
              selected={menu.tag.color || 1}
              onPick={(c) => { saveTag.mutate({ oldName: menu.tag.name, name: menu.tag.name, color: c }); setMenu(null); }}
              onRemove={() => { saveTag.mutate({ oldName: menu.tag.name, name: menu.tag.name, color: 0 }); setMenu(null); }}
            />
          ) : (
            <>
              <button type="button" role="menuitem" className="rc-menu-item" onClick={() => setMenu({ ...menu, colours: true })}>
                <span className="flex h-5 w-5 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-xs" style={{ background: TAG_COLORS[menu.tag.color || 1][1], color: TAG_COLORS[menu.tag.color || 1][0], fontFamily: "Roboto, sans-serif" }}>a</span>
                <span className="ml-[3px] flex-1">Tag Color</span>
                <MIcon name="arrow_right" />
              </button>
              <button type="button" role="menuitem" className="rc-menu-item" onClick={() => { setEditing(menu.tag); setMenu(null); }}>Edit Tag</button>
              <button type="button" role="menuitem" className="rc-menu-item" onClick={() => { setDeleting(menu.tag); setMenu(null); }}>Delete Tag</button>
            </>
          )}
        </PopMenu>
      )}
      {editing && (
        <TagEditDialog
          tag={editing}
          onClose={() => setEditing(null)}
          onSaved={(t) => { if (current === editing.name) navigate(rcTagPath(t.name), { replace: true }); }}
        />
      )}
      {deleting && (
        <ConfirmDialog
          title={`Delete ${deleting.name}`}
          message={`This will delete the tag ${deleting.name}. Are you sure you want to continue?`}
          onNo={() => setDeleting(null)}
          onYes={() => {
            const name = deleting.name;
            setDeleting(null);
            deleteTag.mutate(name, { onSuccess: () => { if (current === name) navigate(RC_TAGS_PATH, { replace: true }); } });
          }}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

1	import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
2	import { useUpsertPassage } from "../hooks/useReadingCenterData";

5	import { MIcon } from "./Icon";
6	
7	/**
8	 * Spreeder "Add To Library": full-screen title + text, Cancel / Save.
9	 * Paste Text opens it empty; Import Document reads a local text file into it;
10	 * Edit Text (card menu) opens it filled.
11	 */
12	export function AddTextScreen({
13	  mode,
14	  passage,
15	  onClose,
16	  onSaved,
17	}: {
18	  mode: "paste" | "import" | "edit";
19	  passage?: Passage;
20	  onClose: () => void;
21	  onSaved: (slug: string) => void;
22	}): React.ReactElement {
23	  const upsert = useUpsertPassage();
24	  const [title, setTitle] = useState(passage?.title ?? "");
25	  const [body, setBody] = useState(passage?.body ?? "");
26	  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
27	  const file = useRef<HTMLInputElement | null>(null);
28	
29	  useEffect(() => {
30	    if (mode === "import") file.current?.click();
31	  }, [mode]);
32	
33	  const readFile = async (f: File) => {
34	    const text = await f.text();
35	    const plain = /\.html?$/i.test(f.name) ? new DOMParser().parseFromString(text, "text/html").body.innerText : text;
36	    setTitle((t) => t || f.name.replace(/\.[^.]+$/, ""));
37	    setBody(plain.trim());
38	  };
39	
40	  const canSave = title.trim() !== "" && body.trim() !== "" && !upsert.isPending;
41	  const save = () =>
42	    upsert.mutate(
43	      passage ? { id: passage.id, title: title.trim(), body: body.trim() } : { title: title.trim(), body: body.trim() },
44	      { onSuccess: (p) => onSaved(p.slug), onError: (e) => setError(e.message) },
45	    );
46	
47	  return (
48	    <Modal open onClose={onClose} fullScreen closeOnEscape labelledBy="rc-add-title">
49	      <div className="flex h-full flex-col px-[30px] pb-5 pt-4">
50	        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
51	          <h2 id="rc-add-title" className="pl-1 text-2xl" style={{ color: "var(--rc-text)" }}>{mode === "edit" ? "Edit Text" : "Add To Library"}</h2>
52	          <button type="button" aria-label="Close" onClick={onClose} style={{ color: "var(--rc-strong)" }}><MIcon name="close" /></button>
53	        </div>
54	        <input
55	          value={title}
56	          onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)}
57	          placeholder="Enter Title"
58	          aria-label="Enter Title"
59	          className="mt-[38px] w-full bg-transparent pb-3 text-[13px] font-medium outline-none placeholder:text-[var(--rc-text)]"
60	          style={{ borderBottom: "1px solid rgba(128,128,128,.5)", color: "var(--rc-text)" }}
61	        />
62	        <textarea
63	          value={body}
64	          onChange={(e) => setBody(e.target.value)}
65	          placeholder="Add Text Here"
66	          aria-label="Add Text Here"
67	          className="mt-[42px] min-h-0 w-full flex-1 resize-none rounded-sm bg-transparent p-4 text-[13px] font-medium outline-none placeholder:text-[var(--rc-text)]"
68	          style={{ border: "1px solid rgba(128,128,128,.6)", color: "var(--rc-text)" }}
69	        />
70	        {error && <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-red-500">{error}</p>}
71	        <div className="mt-8 flex items-center justify-end gap-6 pr-6">
72	          {mode !== "edit" && (

74	              <input ref={file} type="file" accept=".txt,.md,.text,.html,.htm,text/plain,text/markdown,text/html" className="hidden" onChange={(e) => e.target.files?.[0] && void readFile(e.target.files[0])} />

76	                Import Document
77	              </button>
78	            </>

170	 */
171	export function highlightPages(lines: Line[], stageHeight: number, fontSize: number, numberOfLines: number): Page[] {
172	  const linesPerPage = Math.max(1, Math.floor((stageHeight - 20) / fontSize));
173	  const groupsPerPage = Math.max(1, Math.floor(linesPerPage / numberOfLines));
174	  const perPage = groupsPerPage * numberOfLines;
175	  const pages: Page[] = [];
176	  for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i += perPage) {
177	    const pl = lines.slice(i, i + perPage);
178	    const groups: Chunk[] = [];
179	    for (let g = 0; g < pl.length; g += numberOfLines) groups.push(toChunk(pl.slice(g, g + numberOfLines)));
180	    pages.push({ lines: pl, groups });
181	  }
182	  return pages;
183	}
184	
185	// ---------------------------------------------------------------- timing
186	
187	const PUNCTUATION = /[.?!:;\-–,]/;
188	
189	export interface DelayOptions {
190	  wpm: number;
191	  numberOfLines: number;
192	  speedVariability: boolean;
193	  punctuationPauses: boolean;
194	}
195	
196	/** Spreeder computeSwitchingTimeDelays for one section. */
197	export function chunkDelays(section: Section, chunks: Chunk[], opts: DelayOptions): number[] {
198	  const n = chunks.length;
199	  if (n === 0) return [];
200	  const total = Math.round((section.words.length / opts.wpm) * 60000);
201	  const perChunk = Math.round(total / n);
202	  const avgChars = section.text.length / n;
203	  const lastLines = chunks[n - 1].lines.length;
204	  const even = evenChunkDelays(total, n, lastLines, opts.numberOfLines);
205	  return chunks.map((c, i) => {
206	    const notLast = i + 1 < n;
207	    let d = opts.speedVariability ? perChunk * ((c.text.length + (notLast ? 1 : 0)) / avgChars) : notLast ? even.even : even.last;
208	    if (opts.punctuationPauses && PUNCTUATION.test(c.text)) d *= 2;
209	    return d;
210	  });
211	}
212	
213	export function evenChunkDelays(total: number, count: number, lastLines: number, numberOfLines: number): { even: number; last: number } {
214	  if (count <= 1) return { even: total, last: total };
215	  const f = Math.min(Math.max(lastLines / numberOfLines, 0), 1);
216	  const even = total / (count - 1 + f);
217	  return { even, last: even * f };
218	}

/** Spreeder computeTotalTimeBySection: word-count chunks of groupSize * lines, used for "remaining". */
export function sectionTime(text: string, opts: { wpm: number; groupSize: number; numberOfLines: number; punctuationPauses: boolean }): number {
  if (!text) return 0;
  const n = Math.max(1, opts.groupSize * opts.numberOfLines);
  const chunks: string[] = Array.from(text.match(new RegExp(`[\\S]+(?:\\s+[\\S]+){0,${n - 1}}`, "g")) ?? []);
  const totalWords = text.split(" ").length;
  const total = Math.round((totalWords / opts.wpm) * 60000);
  if (!opts.punctuationPauses) return total;
  const per = Math.round(total / chunks.length);
  return chunks.reduce((sum, c) => sum + (PUNCTUATION.test(c) ? 2 * per : per), 0);
}

export function remainingMs(model: TextModel, wordIndex: number, opts: { wpm: number; groupSize: number; numberOfLines: number; punctuationPauses: boolean }): number {
  const word = model.words[wordIndex] ?? model.words[0];
  if (!word) return 0;
  const section = model.sections[word.sectionIndex];
  const offset = word.index - section.words[0].index;
  const rest = section.words.slice(offset).map((w) => w.text).join(" ");
  let ms = sectionTime(rest, opts);
  for (let s = word.sectionIndex + 1; s < model.sections.length; s++) ms += sectionTime(model.sections[s].text, opts);
  return ms;
}

### Side-by-side pass (2026-10-13 afternoon, reading-center @ 2c2f877)

Matched against live Spreeder (1036px window) or its shipped code, then checked in our app:
- Library: heading y75, cover 148x210 r5 at y111, title 12.5/400, author 11.5 #918f8f, View All #74b2e5, shelf gap 50px. Card menu 201 wide, 16px rows, opens above the ⋮ with right edges aligned.
- Sidenav collapses to a 90px icon rail (not hidden). Add Text menu 192 wide, 14/400, 24px icons. AI items left out (Joshua, 2026-10-13).
- Tags: library/tags swaps the nav for Back, Create New Tag and tag rows (⋮: Tag Color with 24 swatches .c1-.c24, Edit Tag, Delete Tag with "This will delete the tag X..." No/Yes). First tag auto-opens; none shows "Nothing here yet." Tag dialog "Tag <title> as": 300px checklist, Create New Tag stacks a dialog.
- Delete Text: 450 wide, "Are you sure you want to delete <title>?", No / Yes (green 120).
- Edit Image: cover 150x225 + Upload Image (Generate With AI left out); Save disabled until changed.
- View/Location (library and reader): a word click moves the location and keeps the panel open.
- Add To Library: Enter Title focused, outlined text box, Cancel + Save 120 (disabled grey). Edit Text: Title, Author, Genre, text, one green Save.
- Bookmarks (from code): list grouped under the text title, 300px bordered table "Page N - Word" (#448aff) | notes | ⋮ (Go to location, Delete). Note dialog: chips title/"Page N - Word", "Position <charPosition>", word; autosaves 5s after typing ("Bookmark saved."); Save (create) / Update + Go to location. No Cancel or Delete in it.
- Word action sheet: #1f1f1f, title 60px 14px #666, rows 52px 14px.
- Bottom bar at 1180px and below: keyboard hidden, time left, speed + View right.
- Punctuation regex as shipped, /[\\.\\?!:;\\-\\\u2013,]/, does NOT match the ASCII hyphen. Fixed in both delays and remaining time; "3% - 2 minutes remaining" now matches Spreeder exactly.
- Speed Variability formula confirmed against shipped serial/flow code; unit-tested.
- 8px scrollbar (white track, #9b9b9b thumb) from styles.css.

Still not verified: Spreeder's own Import Document UI (it opens a file picker), finish alert, fullscreen (needs a real click), High Contrast and System themes, the shortcuts popover against live Spreeder at >1180px (Joshua's window is 1036px).

## assistant

You've hit your usage limit, so I stopped here. Most of the untested features are now compared side by side with real Spreeder and fixed. Typecheck is clean, all 30 tests pass, and the work is committed as `2c2f877` in the standalone app.

**Your decisions, applied:**
- **Flow:** keeps the left-to-right reveal.
- **Outside services:** AI Book Summary and AI Generate are removed from the Add Text menu. Look Up Word and the Wordela list stay out.
- **Profile name:** stays "Joshua".
- **LSAT U worktree:** `supabase/functions/mcp/index.ts` is restored and the worktree is clean.

**Fixed to match Spreeder, each checked in our app:**
- **Timing bug:** Spreeder's punctuation pause does not count the plain hyphen (a quirk in its shipped code). Ours did, so we showed "3 minutes remaining" where Spreeder showed "2". Both apps now read "3% - 2 minutes remaining". This also changes word timing.
- **Tags:** this was missing almost entirely. Built:
  - Spreeder's Tags mode, where the menu switches to Back, Create New Tag and the tag list.
  - Each tag's ⋮ menu: 24 tag colours, Edit Tag, and Delete Tag with a confirm.
  - The "Tag <title> as" checklist and the Create New Tag dialog.
- **Bookmarks:** rebuilt from Spreeder's code.
  - The list shows "Page N - Word", the note, and a ⋮ menu with Go to location and Delete.
  - The note dialog saves on its own 5 seconds after you stop typing.
  - The button reads Save for a new bookmark and Update for an existing one.
- **Library:**
  - Card sizes and spacing.
  - The card ⋮ menu size and position.
  - Delete Text confirm, and Edit Image (upload only).
  - In View/Location, clicking a word now moves your place and keeps the panel open, in the library and the reader. Ours used to jump into the reader or close the panel.
  - The collapsed menu is now a narrow 90px icon strip, not hidden.
- **Text screens:** Add To Library puts the cursor in the title and has a 120px Save. Edit Text now has Author and Genre fields.
- **Smaller fixes:** the word pop-up menu sizes, and the bottom bar's narrow-window layout (at 1180px and below the keyboard button hides). Also dialog titles no longer wrap, and the scrollbar is Spreeder's 8px one.

**Not fully verified:**
- **Inferred from Spreeder's code, not seen live:** the filled bookmark list and the filled tag list. Making them live would have meant adding data to your real account.
- **Not checked against Spreeder:**
  - Its Import Document screen (it opens a file picker). Ours works when fed a file directly.
  - The finish alert.
  - Fullscreen (needs a real click).
  - High Contrast and System themes.
  - The shortcuts popover. It only appears in windows wider than 1180px, and your Chrome window is 1036px. Ours works and all the keys work.
- **Small quirk:** pressing ↑ and + in the same instant adds 10 WPM, not 20. Normal separate presses work.

**Side effects on your Spreeder account:** two misclicks opened texts in the reader. That only reordered Recent, and both texts are still at 0% and 3%. Nothing was created, saved or deleted, and I closed the Chrome tab I opened.

**Still to do:** check the items above against Spreeder and look over the Profile screens again. After that comes moving the app into LSAT U. All the measurements are in section 7 of `research/2026-10-13-spreeder-reconstruction-spec.md`. The dev server is still running on port 8100.
